class ConfigManager
{

static bool validate(const Json::Value &config, std::string &errorMessage);

static const char *getEnv(const char *name);
private:

};

;
}

=== libs/common/include/fulla/common/error/ErrorCatalog.h
#pragma once
#include <fulla/common/error/ErrorTypes.h>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>
namespace fulla::common::error
{
struct CatalogEntry
{
std::string_view code;
int numericCode;
ErrorCategory category;
int httpStatus;
std::string_view
defaultMessage;
std::string_view description;
};
struct OAuthCatalogEntry
{
std::string_view error;
int httpStatus;
std::string_view defaultErrorDesc;
std::string_view errorUri;
};
struct NumericSegment
{
int min;
int max;
};
class ErrorCatalog
{
public:
static const CatalogEntry *find(std::string_view code);
static const CatalogEntry *findByNumeric(int numericCode);
static const OAuthCatalogEntry *findOAuth(std::string_view error);
static const std::vector<CatalogEntry> &allEntries();
static const std::vector<OAuthCatalogEntry> &allOAuthEntries();
static const CatalogEntry &internalError();
static NumericSegment segmentFor(ErrorCategory category);
static void validateInvariants();
};
}

=== libs/common/include/fulla/common/error/ErrorTypes.h
#pragma once
#include <exception>
#include <string>
#include <json/json.h>
namespace fulla::common::error
{
enum class ErrorCategory
{
NETWORK,
DATABASE,
VALIDATION,
AUTHENTICATION,
AUTHORIZATION,
INTERNAL,
UNKNOWN
};
const char *toString(ErrorCategory category);
struct Error
{
std::string code;
ErrorCategory category;
std::string message;
std::string details;
std::string requestId;
int toHttpStatusCode() const;
bool hasNumericCode() const;
int numericCode() const;
Json::Value toJson(bool includeDetails) const;
static Error fromCode(std::string code, std::string requestId);
static Error fromException(
const std::exception &e,
ErrorCategory category,
std::string requestId
);
};
}

=== libs/common/include/fulla/common/result/Result.h
#pragma once
#include <fulla/common/error/ErrorTypes.h>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <utility>
#include <variant>
namespace fulla::common::result
{
class BadResultAccess : public std::logic_error
{
public:
explicit BadResultAccess(const char *what) : std::logic_error(what)
;
};
template <typename T, typename E = fulla::common::error::Error>
class Result
{
public:
static Result ok(T value)
;
static Result err(E error)
;
bool ok() const noexcept
;
bool isError() const noexcept
;
explicit operator bool() const noexcept
;
const T &value() const &
;
T &value() &
;
T &&value() &&
;
const E &error() const &
;
E &error() &
;
E &&error() &&
;
T valueOr(T fallback) const &
;
T valueOr(T fallback) &&
;
private:
template <typename... Args>
explicit Result(std::in_place_index_t<0> tag, Args &&...args)
: storage_(tag, std::forward<Args>(args)...)
;
template <typename... Args>
explicit Result(std::in_place_index_t<1> tag, Args &&...args)
: storage_(tag, std::forward<Args>(args)...)
;
std::variant<T, E> storage_;
};
}

=== libs/common/include/fulla/common/utils/RateLimiter.h
#pragma once
#include <chrono>
#include <deque>
#include <mutex>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <unordered_map>
namespace fulla::common::utils
{
struct RateLimiterConfig
{
std::size_t maxFailures = 30;
std::chrono::seconds windowSeconds{60};
std::size_t maxBuckets = 10000;
static RateLimiterConfig defaults() noexcept
;
};
class RateLimiter
{
public:
static RateLimiter &instance()
;
void configure(const RateLimiterConfig &cfg)
;
RateLimiterConfig getConfig()
;
std::chrono::seconds checkThrottled(std::string_view key)
;
void recordFailure(std::string_view key)
;
void recordSuccess(std::string_view key)
;
void reset()
;
private:
RateLimiter() = default;
using TimePoint = std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point;
void purgeOldLocked(std::deque<TimePoint> &bucket, const TimePoint &now)
;
void sweepLocked(const TimePoint &now)
;
std::mutex mutex_;
RateLimiterConfig config_{RateLimiterConfig::defaults()};
std::unordered_map<std::string, std::deque<TimePoint>> buckets_;
};
}

=== libs/drogon/include/fulla/drogon/authz/ResourceScopeRegistry.h
#pragma once
#include <fulla/drogon/observability/openapi/OpenApiGenerator.h>
#include <drogon/HttpTypes.h>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>
namespace fulla::drogon::authz
{
enum class ScopeMatch
{
All,
Any
};
struct ResourceScopeRequirement
{
std::vector<std::string> scopes;
ScopeMatch match = ScopeMatch::All;
std::vector<std::string> impliedBy;
};
class ResourceScopeRegistry
{
public:
struct Entry
{
std::string path;
std::string method;
ResourceScopeRequirement requirement;
};
static void buildFromEndpoints();
static void registerPrefix(const std::string &prefix, const ResourceScopeRequirement &req);
static const ResourceScopeRequirement *lookup(
std::string_view path, ::drogon::HttpMethod method);
static std::vector<Entry> snapshot();
static void runConsistencyCheck();
static void clear();
static bool isBuilt();
static void clearPrefixes();
};
}

;

=== libs/drogon/include/fulla/drogon/observability/openapi/OpenApiGenerator.h
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <map>
#include <json/json.h>
namespace fulla::drogon::observability::openapi
{
enum class ParameterType
{
STRING,
INTEGER,
NUMBER,
BOOLEAN,
ARRAY,
OBJECT
};
enum class ParameterLocation
{
QUERY,
HEADER,
PATH,
COOKIE
};
struct ParameterInfo
{
std::string name;
std::string description;
ParameterType type = ParameterType::STRING;
ParameterLocation location = ParameterLocation::QUERY;
bool required = true;
std::string defaultValue;
std::string enumValues;
std::string format;
};
enum class AuthType
{
Bearer,
ClientCredentials
};
struct EndpointInfo
{
std::string path;
std::string method;
std::string summary;
std::string description;
std::vector<std::string> tags;
std::vector<ParameterInfo> parameters;
std::map<int, std::string> responses;
std::map<int, Json::Value> responseExamples;
bool requiresAuth;
AuthType authType = AuthType::Bearer;
std::vector<std::string> requiredScopes;
std::vector<std::string> impliedBy;
};
class OpenApiGenerator
{
public:
static void addEndpoint(const EndpointInfo &endpoint);
static Json::Value generateOpenApiSpec();
static bool writeToFile(const std::string &outputPath);
static void setApiInfo(
const std::string &title,
const std::string &version,
const std::string &description
);
static void setServerConfig(const std::string &url, const std::string &description = "");
static std::string parameterTypeToString(ParameterType type);
static std::string parameterLocationToString(ParameterLocation location);
static const std::vector<EndpointInfo> &endpoints();
private:
static std::vector<EndpointInfo> &getEndpoints();
static Json::Value &getApiInfo();
static bool &getInitialized();
static Json::Value &getServerConfig();
static Json::Value generatePathItem(const EndpointInfo &endpoint);
static Json::Value generateParameter(const ParameterInfo &param);
static Json::Value generateSchema();
};
}

=== libs/drogon/include/fulla/drogon/validation/Rule.h
#pragma once
#include <string>
#include <functional>
namespace fulla::drogon::validation
{
inline const char *CLIENT_ID_PATTERN = "^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{1,128}$";
inline const size_t CLIENT_ID_MIN_LEN = 1;
inline const size_t CLIENT_ID_MAX_LEN = 128;
inline const char *REDIRECT_URI_PATTERN =
"^https?://(\\[[a-fA-F0-9:.]+\\]|[a-zA-Z0-9.-]+)(:[0-9]+)?(/[^\\s]*)?$";
inline const size_t REDIRECT_URI_MIN_LEN = 10;
inline const size_t REDIRECT_URI_MAX_LEN = 2048;
inline const char *SCOPE_PATTERN = "^[a-zA-Z0-9: ]+$";
inline const size_t SCOPE_MIN_LEN = 1;
inline const size_t SCOPE_MAX_LEN = 256;
inline const char *TOKEN_PATTERN = "^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+$";
inline const size_t TOKEN_MIN_LEN = 32;
inline const char *RESPONSE_TYPE_PATTERN = "^[a-zA-Z0-9_]+$";
inline const char *GRANT_TYPE_PATTERN = "^[a-zA-Z0-9_]+$";
inline const char *USERNAME_PATTERN = "^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,100}$";
inline const char *PASSWORD_PATTERN = "^[a-zA-Z0-9!@#$%^&*()_+]{8,200}$";
inline const char *EMAIL_PATTERN =
"^[a-zA-Z0-9.!#$%&'*+/=?^_`{|}~-]+@[a-zA-Z0-9]"
"(?:[a-zA-Z0-9-]{0,61}[a-zA-Z0-9])?"
"(?:\\.[a-zA-Z0-9](?:[a-zA-Z0-9-]{0,61}[a-zA-Z0-9])?)*$";
inline const size_t EMAIL_MAX_LEN = 254;
enum class RuleType
{
NotEmpty,
LengthLimit,
Regex,
NumericRange,
UrlFormat,
EmailFormat
};
struct Rule
{
std::string field;
std::string source;
bool required;
size_t minLength = 0;
size_t maxLength = 0;
std::string pattern;
std::function<bool(const std::string &)> custom;
};
struct Result
{
bool ok;
std::string field;
std::string message;
static Result success();
static Result failure(const std::string &field, const std::string &message);
};
}
=== libs/drogon/include/fulla/drogon/validation/RuleEngine.h
#pragma once
#include <fulla/drogon/validation/Rule.h>
#include <vector>
#include <utility>
namespace fulla::drogon::validation
{
class RuleEngine
{
public:
static Result notEmpty(const std::string &value, const std::string &fieldName);
static Result length(
const std::string &value,
const std::string &fieldName,
size_t minLen,
size_t maxLen
);
static Result regex(
const std::string &value,
const std::string &fieldName,
const std::string &pattern
);
static Result numericRange(int value, const std::string &fieldName, int minVal, int maxVal);
static Result validateClientId(const std::string &clientId);
static Result validateClientSecret(const std::string &secret);
static Result validateRedirectUri(const std::string &uri);
static Result validateScope(const std::string &scope);
static Result validateResponseType(const std::string &type);
static Result validateGrantType(const std::string &type);
static Result validateToken(const std::string &token);
static std::vector<Result> validateAll(
const std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> &fieldsAndValues,
const std::vector<RuleType> &rules
);
};
}

=== libs/identity/include/fulla/identity/ISocialAccountRepository.h
#pragma once
#ifdef WITH_SOCIAL
#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
namespace fulla::identity
{
enum class SocialLinkStatus
{
Linked,
NoMapping,
AccountUnavailable,
RepositoryError
};
struct SocialAccountLookup
{
int32_t userId = 0;
std::string username;
std::string publicSub;
};
struct LinkNewSocialAccountResult
{
int32_t userId = 0;
std::string username;
std::string publicSub;
};
struct SocialLinkEntry
{
std::string provider;
std::string subject;
std::string linkedAt;
};
enum class LinkMutationStatus
{
Inserted,
Conflict,
Deleted,
NoLink,
Error
};
class ISocialAccountRepository
{
public:
virtual ~ISocialAccountRepository() = default;
using LookupCallback =
std::function<void(SocialLinkStatus status, const SocialAccountLookup &lookup)>;
using CreateCallback = std::function<void(std::optional<LinkNewSocialAccountResult>)>;
virtual void findLinkedUser(
const std::string &provider,
const std::string &subject,
LookupCallback &&cb
) = 0;
virtual void createLinkedUser(
const std::string &provider,
const std::string &subject,
const std::string &username,
const std::string &email,
CreateCallback &&cb
) = 0;
using LinkEntriesCallback = std::function<void(std::optional<std::vector<SocialLinkEntry>>)>;
using LinkMutationCallback = std::function<void(LinkMutationStatus)>;
using PasswordUsableCallback = std::function<void(std::optional<bool>)>;
virtual void listForUser(int32_t internalUserId, LinkEntriesCallback &&cb) = 0;
virtual void insertLink(
const std::string &provider,
const std::string &subject,
int32_t internalUserId,
LinkMutationCallback &&cb
) = 0;
virtual void deleteLink(
const std::string &provider,
int32_t internalUserId,
LinkMutationCallback &&cb
) = 0;
virtual void userHasUsablePassword(int32_t internalUserId, PasswordUsableCallback &&cb) = 0;
};
}
#endif

) = 0;

) = 0;

#pragma once

Error
};

{

};

{

};

{

const std::string &name,

) = 0;

const std::string &credentialId,

) = 0;

const std::string &credentialId,

) = 0;

const std::string &credentialId,
int expectedCurrent,
int newSignCount,
BoolCallback &&cb
)
;
virtual void listCredentials(int32_t userId, ListCredentialsCallback &&cb) = 0;
};

{
void operator()(EVP_PKEY *key) const
;
};

;
inline EvpPkeyPtr generateP256()
;

;

;

;

=== libs/oauth2/include/fulla/oauth2/model/Dto.h
#pragma once
#include <fulla/oauth2/model/ClientType.h>
#include <json/json.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
namespace fulla::oauth2::model
{
struct OAuth2Client
{
std::string clientId;
ClientType clientType;
std::string clientSecretHash;
std::string salt;
std::vector<std::string> redirectUris;
std::vector<std::string> allowedScopes;
std::string tokenEndpointAuthMethod;
std::vector<std::string> allowedGrantTypes;
};
struct OAuth2AuthCode
{
std::string code;
std::string clientId;
std::string userId;
std::string scope;
std::string redirectUri;
std::string codeChallenge;
std::string codeChallengeMethod;
std::string nonce;
int64_t expiresAt;
bool used = false;
int64_t authTime = 0;
std::string amr;
std::optional<int32_t> orgId;
};
struct OAuth2AccessToken
{
std::string token;
std::string clientId;
std::string userId;
std::string scope;
int64_t expiresAt;
bool revoked = false;
int64_t issuedAt = 0;
std::string issuer;
std::string audience;
int64_t notBefore = 0;
int introspectCount = 0;
int64_t revokedAt = 0;
std::string revokedBy;
std::optional<int32_t> orgId;
};
struct OAuth2RefreshToken
{
std::string token;
std::string accessToken;
std::string clientId;
std::string userId;
std::string scope;
int64_t expiresAt;
bool revoked = false;
std::string familyId;
int64_t revokedAt = 0;
std::string revokedBy;
std::optional<int32_t> orgId;
};
struct TokenIntrospection
{
bool active = false;
std::string clientId;
std::string tokenType = "Bearer";
int64_t exp = 0;
int64_t iat = 0;
int64_t nbf = 0;
std::string sub;
std::string aud;
std::string iss;
std::string scope;
std::optional<int32_t> orgId;
Json::Value toJson() const
;
};
struct AuthorizationTransaction
{
std::string transactionId;
std::string clientId;
std::string subject;
std::string redirectUri;
std::string state;
std::string codeChallenge;
std::string codeChallengeMethod;
std::vector<std::string> requestedScopes;
std::vector<std::string> validScopes;
std::vector<std::string> consentRequiredScopes;
bool consumed = false;
int64_t expiresAt;
};
}

=== libs/oauth2/include/fulla/oauth2/repository/ITokenRepository.h
#pragma once
#include <fulla/oauth2/model/Dto.h>
#include <functional>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
namespace fulla::oauth2::repository
{
class ITokenRepository
{
public:
virtual ~ITokenRepository() = default;
using AccessTokenCallback =
std::function<void(std::optional<fulla::oauth2::model::OAuth2AccessToken>)>;
using RefreshTokenCallback =
std::function<void(std::optional<fulla::oauth2::model::OAuth2RefreshToken>)>;
using VoidCallback = std::function<void()>;
using TokenIntrospectionCallback =
std::function<void(std::optional<fulla::oauth2::model::TokenIntrospection>)>;
using SaveResultCallback = std::function<void(bool ok)>;
virtual void saveAccessToken(
const fulla::oauth2::model::OAuth2AccessToken &token,
VoidCallback &&cb
) = 0;
virtual void getAccessToken(const std::string &token, AccessTokenCallback &&cb) = 0;
virtual void saveTokenPair(
const fulla::oauth2::model::OAuth2AccessToken &at,
const fulla::oauth2::model::OAuth2RefreshToken &rt,
SaveResultCallback &&cb
)
;
virtual void saveRefreshToken(
const fulla::oauth2::model::OAuth2RefreshToken &token,
VoidCallback &&cb
) = 0;
virtual void getRefreshToken(const std::string &token, RefreshTokenCallback &&cb) = 0;
virtual void revokeRefreshToken(const std::string &token, VoidCallback &&cb) = 0;
virtual void atomicRevokeRefreshToken(const std::string &token, RefreshTokenCallback &&cb) = 0;
virtual void revokeTokenFamily(const std::string &familyId, VoidCallback &&cb) = 0;
virtual void introspectToken(const std::string &token, TokenIntrospectionCallback &&cb) = 0;
virtual void incrementIntrospectCount(const std::string &token, VoidCallback &&cb) = 0;
virtual void revokeAccessToken(
const std::string &token,
const std::string &revokedBy,
VoidCallback &&cb
) = 0;
virtual void purgeExpired() = 0;
virtual bool supportsTransactions() const = 0;
virtual bool supportsCas() const = 0;
};
}

=== libs/storage-postgres/include/fulla/storage/postgres/ClientOwnersRepository.h
#pragma once
#include <drogon/orm/DbClient.h>
#include <fulla/storage/postgres/models/Oauth2ClientOwners.h>
#include <fulla/storage/postgres/models/OrganizationMembers.h>
#include <fulla/storage/postgres/models/Organizations.h>
#include <functional>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
namespace fulla::storage::postgres
{
enum class LookupStatus
{
Found,
NoRow,
Error
};
struct OwnerRowLookup
{
LookupStatus status = LookupStatus::Error;
::drogon_model::fulla_db::Oauth2ClientOwners row{};
std::string error;
};
struct MembershipLookup
{
LookupStatus status = LookupStatus::Error;
::drogon_model::fulla_db::OrganizationMembers row{};
std::string error;
};
struct OrganizationLookup
{
LookupStatus status = LookupStatus::Error;
::drogon_model::fulla_db::Organizations row{};
std::string error;
};
using OwnerRowCallback = std::function<void(const OwnerRowLookup &)>;
using MembershipCallback = std::function<void(const MembershipLookup &)>;
using OwnerLabelCallback = std::function<void(const std::string &)>;
class ClientOwnersRepository
{
public:
explicit ClientOwnersRepository(::drogon::orm::DbClientPtr dbClient);
void findOwnerRow(const std::string &clientId, OwnerRowCallback &&cb);
void findMembership(int32_t orgId, int32_t userId, MembershipCallback &&cb);
void findOrganization(const std::string &orgRef, std::function<void(const OrganizationLookup &)> &&cb);
void findUserIdBySubject(
const std::string &subject,
std::function<void(std::optional<int32_t>)> &&cb
);
void resolveOwnerLabel(const std::string &clientId, OwnerLabelCallback &&cb);
private:
::drogon::orm::DbClientPtr dbClient_;
};
}

void outputArgs(drogon::orm::internal::SqlBinder &binder) const;
const std::vector<std::string> updateColumns() const;
void updateArgs(drogon::orm::internal::SqlBinder &binder) const;
void updateId(const uint64_t id);
std::shared_ptr<int32_t> id_;
std::shared_ptr<int32_t> userId_;
std::shared_ptr<std::string> credentialId_;
std::shared_ptr<std::string> publicKey_;
std::shared_ptr<int32_t> signCount_;
std::shared_ptr<std::string> transports_;
std::shared_ptr<std::string> name_;
std::shared_ptr<::trantor::Date> createdAt_;
std::shared_ptr<::trantor::Date> lastUsedAt_;
struct MetaData
{
const std::string colName_;
const std::string colType_;
const std::string colDatabaseType_;
const ssize_t colLength_;
const bool isAutoVal_;
const bool isPrimaryKey_;
const bool notNull_;
};
static const std::vector<MetaData> metaData_;
bool dirtyFlag_[9]={ false };
public:
static const std::string &sqlForFindingByPrimaryKey()
;
static const std::string &sqlForDeletingByPrimaryKey()
;
std::string sqlForInserting(bool &needSelection) const
;
};
}
